# Kivro Requirements Catalog

> Generated from `MASTER-SPEC.md` as a traceability layer.
> `MASTER-SPEC.md` remains authoritative.

## Rules

-   Every Master Spec section has at least one `SECTION_COVERAGE`
    requirement.
-   Normative statements are additionally extracted as atomic
    traceability items.
-   If wording here conflicts with the Master Spec, the Master Spec
    wins.
-   Codex must not mark a section-level requirement complete until all
    behavior in that source section is accounted for.
-   `P0` = security/payment correctness; `P1` = required MVP behavior;
    `P2` = required quality/UX/operations; `P3` is reserved only for
    explicitly post-MVP work.

    visual shortcuts that make a product immediately look AI-generated.

## Curated requirements --- Local development environment

  ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ID              Priority   Source   Requirement
  --------------- ---------- -------- ---------------------------------------------------------
  DEV-LOCAL-001   P1         §551     Kivro must be fully runnable and meaningfully testable on
                                      a supported developer Mac without production cloud
                                      infrastructure.

  DEV-LOCAL-002   P1         §552     Docker Compose local infrastructure must include
                                      PostgreSQL, Redis and S3-compatible object storage.

  DEV-LOCAL-003   P1         §553     PostgreSQL must use Docker with pinned version, health
                                      check and persistent volume; no host PostgreSQL
                                      installation required.

  DEV-LOCAL-004   P1         §555     Use MinIO or equivalent S3-compatible local storage and
                                      automatically create required buckets.

  DEV-LOCAL-005   P1         §556     Kivro Worker must run host-native and independently
                                      observable during local development.

  DEV-LOCAL-006   P1         §557     Full local OpenClaw E2E acceptance must use a real
                                      supported OpenClaw runtime; lower test layers may mock
                                      adapters.

  DEV-LOCAL-007   P0         §558     Development buyer jobs must execute in the real sandbox
                                      architecture and never directly on host.

  DEV-LOCAL-008   P0         §558     Sandbox unavailable must fail closed; development must
                                      never fall back to host execution.

  DEV-LOCAL-009   P1         §559     Provide idempotent pnpm dev:setup that starts
                                      dependencies, waits for health, migrates, creates buckets
                                      and seeds.

  DEV-LOCAL-010   P1         §560     Provide pnpm dev for cloud-side services and pnpm
                                      worker:dev for the host-native Worker.

  DEV-LOCAL-011   P1         §561     Provide deterministic pnpm dev:demo or equivalent with
                                      buyer, seller, credits, Worker bootstrap and
                                      representative capabilities.

  DEV-LOCAL-012   P0         §561     Development-only shortcuts must be impossible to enable
                                      accidentally in production.

  DEV-LOCAL-013   P1         §562     One machine must simulate distinct buyer and seller
                                      identities and browser sessions.

  DEV-LOCAL-014   P1         §563     Local environment must support the complete
                                      buyer-to-seller job path through payment, dispatch,
                                      Worker, sandbox, OpenClaw, storage, result and ledger.

  DEV-LOCAL-015   P1         §564     Provide deterministic fake development payment adapter
                                      plus Stripe test-mode integration path.

  DEV-LOCAL-016   P0         §564     Fake payment adapter must be impossible to enable in
                                      production.

  DEV-LOCAL-017   P1         §565     Development financial flows must use the same production
                                      ledger/domain logic.

  DEV-LOCAL-018   P1         §566     Provide safe environment templates and configurable local
                                      service endpoints without real secrets.

  DEV-LOCAL-019   P0         §566     Production must fail closed if development-only
                                      flags/adapters are enabled.

  DEV-LOCAL-020   P1         §567     Expose clear readiness for DB, Redis, storage, API,
                                      Worker, OpenClaw and sandbox.

  DEV-LOCAL-021   P2         §568     Provide documented
                                      stop/restart/reset/storage-reset/queue-reset/full-clean
                                      workflows.

  DEV-LOCAL-022   P1         §569     Local logs must correlate one job across all major
                                      components.

  DEV-LOCAL-023   P1         §570     Provide repeatable pnpm test:e2e:local covering the
                                      successful local paid/test-paid job flow.

  DEV-LOCAL-024   P1         §570     Local E2E must cover Worker offline, sandbox unavailable,
                                      payment failure, cancellation, invalid output and
                                      timeout.

  DEV-LOCAL-025   P1         §571     Fresh clone must require no manual host installation of
                                      PostgreSQL, Redis or S3 infrastructure.

  DEV-LOCAL-026   P1         §571     Bootstrap/doctor must detect missing
                                      OpenClaw/prerequisites and provide actionable
                                      instructions.

  DEV-LOCAL-027   P0         §572     Local development must preserve sandbox, identity, path,
                                      SSRF, network and payment security boundaries.

  DEV-LOCAL-028   P0         §572     Local development must not use production credentials,
                                      databases or buckets by default.

  DEV-LOCAL-029   P1         §573     Local development is complete only when the §573
                                      Definition of Done checklist is satisfied and represented
                                      in coverage.

## Curated requirements --- Authentication

  -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ID         Priority   Source     Requirement
  ---------- ---------- ---------- ----------------------------------------
  AUTH-001   P1         §574       Support both email/password and Google
                                   OAuth/OIDC authentication in the MVP.

  AUTH-002   P1         §574       Both authentication methods must resolve
                                   to the same Kivro account and permission
                                   model.

  AUTH-003   P0         §575       Email/password registration must require
                                   verified ownership of the email before
                                   sensitive or paid product actions are
                                   allowed.

  AUTH-004   P0         §575       Passwords must use a modern secure
                                   password hashing implementation and must
                                   never be stored in plaintext.

  AUTH-005   P0         §575       Email verification tokens must be
                                   single-purpose, expiring, securely
                                   generated/signed, and invalid after
                                   successful use.

  AUTH-006   P1         §575       Support rate-limited verification email
                                   resend and clear verification-required
                                   UI.

  AUTH-007   P0         §575       Authentication flows should avoid
                                   unnecessary account enumeration.

  AUTH-008   P1         §576       Google sign-in must use the current
                                   recommended OAuth 2.0/OIDC flow of the
                                   selected authentication framework.

  AUTH-009   P0         §576       Google login must request only identity
                                   scopes required for authentication;
                                   unrelated Google product scopes are
                                   forbidden in the login flow.

  AUTH-010   P0         §576       A Google email may be trusted as
                                   verified only from an authoritative
                                   verified-email provider claim.

  AUTH-011   P0         §577       A verified email/password account and
                                   Google identity with the same
                                   authoritative verified email must
                                   resolve safely to one Kivro account
                                   rather than duplicate accounts.

  AUTH-012   P0         §577       Account linking must be resistant to
                                   takeover and must never rely solely on
                                   an unverified email claim.

  AUTH-013   P1         §577       Persist external provider identity IDs
                                   so linked Google accounts resolve
                                   deterministically.

  AUTH-014   P2         §577       If enabled by product policy, a
                                   Google-origin account can establish a
                                   password through an explicit secure flow
                                   without creating a second account.

  AUTH-015   P1         §578       Provide conventional minimal sign-in UX
                                   with Continue with Google and
                                   email/password entry.

  AUTH-016   P1         §578       Handle invalid credentials, verification
                                   required/sent/expired, resend, Google
                                   cancellation/failure, and account-link
                                   resolution states.

  AUTH-017   P0         §579       Protect authentication against brute
                                   force/credential stuffing, OAuth
                                   CSRF/state attacks, session fixation,
                                   open redirects, verification/reset
                                   abuse, and account-link takeover.

  AUTH-018   P0         §579       Use secure session-cookie settings
                                   appropriate to the deployment and never
                                   log authentication
                                   secrets/tokens/passwords.

  AUTH-019   P1         §579       Implement secure expiring single-purpose
                                   password reset for first-party password
                                   accounts.

  AUTH-020   P1         §579       Audit security-relevant authentication
                                   events without sensitive credential
                                   material.

  AUTH-021   P0         §579       Development authentication shortcuts
                                   must be impossible to enable
                                   accidentally in production.

  AUTH-022   P1         §580       Local development must exercise real
                                   email verification-token logic through a
                                   local/dev email delivery mechanism.

  AUTH-023   P1         §580       Document local Google OAuth redirect
                                   configuration and test real Google
                                   authentication in a non-production
                                   environment.

  AUTH-024   P1         §580       Automated authentication E2E coverage
                                   must include email
                                   registration/verification/login/reset,
                                   Google login behavior, account
                                   deduplication/linking, logout and
                                   failure states.
  -------------------------------------------------------------------------

## Curated requirements --- Netsons production profile

  ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ID             Priority       Source         Requirement
  -------------- -------------- -------------- ---------------------------
  HOST-NET-001   P0             §581           Kivro Cloud production must
                                               deploy on Netsons Pro
                                               Business without a VPS or
                                               separate application
                                               server.

  HOST-NET-002   P0             §582           Production must not require
                                               root, cloud-host Docker,
                                               Kubernetes, systemd,
                                               PM2/Supervisor daemons,
                                               self-hosted DB/Redis/web
                                               servers, BullMQ consumer
                                               daemons, or arbitrary
                                               persistent SSH-launched
                                               processes.

  HOST-NET-003   P1             §583           Use the
                                               Netsons/cPanel-managed
                                               Node.js application
                                               lifecycle with explicit
                                               startup entry point and
                                               environment configuration.

  HOST-NET-004   P1             §583           Prefer the minimum managed
                                               Node app count; where
                                               practical Web, API and
                                               Worker WSS share one
                                               managed Node application.

  HOST-NET-005   P1             §584           Next.js must run as a
                                               conventional managed Node
                                               server without Vercel, edge
                                               runtime, Docker or a custom
                                               reverse proxy.

  HOST-NET-006   P0             §585           Use Netsons-managed
                                               PostgreSQL as the durable
                                               production source of truth.

  HOST-NET-007   P1             §585           Use a bounded PostgreSQL
                                               connection pool suitable
                                               for managed/shared hosting.

  HOST-NET-008   P0             §586           Redis is an
                                               optimization/coordination
                                               layer only; correctness
                                               must survive Redis data
                                               loss.

  HOST-NET-009   P0             §587           Production correctness must
                                               not depend on BullMQ
                                               workers or custom
                                               persistent queue-consumer
                                               daemons.

  HOST-NET-010   P0             §588           Represent all cloud
                                               asynchronous work durably
                                               in PostgreSQL with retry,
                                               claim and idempotency
                                               state.

  HOST-NET-011   P0             §589           Commit paid job state in
                                               PostgreSQL before sending
                                               the Worker WebSocket
                                               signal.

  HOST-NET-012   P0             §589           WebSocket signaling is
                                               never the source of truth;
                                               lost signals recover from
                                               PostgreSQL.

  HOST-NET-013   P1             §590           Worker realtime transport
                                               uses authenticated WSS with
                                               heartbeat, bounded
                                               messages, reconnect,
                                               backoff and reconciliation.

  HOST-NET-014   P0             §591           Worker reconnect reconciles
                                               running, offered, claimed
                                               and completed jobs plus
                                               device/capability state.

  HOST-NET-015   P1             §592           Background maintenance and
                                               retries run through bounded
                                               idempotent cron entry
                                               points.

  HOST-NET-016   P1             §593           Correctness must not
                                               require sub-minute cron
                                               execution.

  HOST-NET-017   P1             §594           Request-driven
                                               opportunistic work is
                                               allowed only after durable
                                               commit and must remain
                                               bounded.

  HOST-NET-018   P0             §595           Buyer webhook delivery is
                                               durable in PostgreSQL and
                                               retryable by cron with
                                               at-least-once semantics.

  HOST-NET-019   P0             §596           Stripe/payment correctness
                                               must not depend on
                                               persistent queue daemons.

  HOST-NET-020   P1             §597           Long-lived Marketplace
                                               Agent/orchestration state
                                               persists between steps
                                               rather than relying on one
                                               in-memory process.

  HOST-NET-021   P1             §598           Use external S3-compatible
                                               object storage for durable
                                               production assets.

  HOST-NET-022   P1             §598           Prefer direct/presigned
                                               object-storage transfer for
                                               large buyer files.

  HOST-NET-023   P1             §599           Use bounded pools, batches,
                                               messages, memory, timeouts,
                                               pagination, rate limits and
                                               backpressure.

  HOST-NET-024   P0             §600           Managed Node process
                                               restart must not lose or
                                               duplicate paid jobs,
                                               payments, payouts, webhooks
                                               or scheduled work.

  HOST-NET-025   P0             §601           Production secrets are
                                               hosting-configured, never
                                               committed, and
                                               development-only defaults
                                               fail closed.

  HOST-NET-026   P1             §602           Transactional email works
                                               through Netsons SMTP or an
                                               external provider behind an
                                               abstraction.

  HOST-NET-027   P1             §603           Document a rootless Netsons
                                               deployment covering Node,
                                               PostgreSQL, Redis,
                                               migrations, cron, TLS, WSS,
                                               Stripe, Google OAuth and
                                               email.

  HOST-NET-028   P1             §604           Provide a safe production
                                               health/readiness endpoint.

  HOST-NET-029   P0             §605           Actual Netsons smoke
                                               testing verifies Node/Next
                                               SSR, PostgreSQL, Redis,
                                               WSS/reconnect, cron,
                                               Stripe, Google OAuth, email
                                               and object storage.

  HOST-NET-030   P0             §606           A real staging
                                               buyer-to-seller E2E on
                                               Netsons is required before
                                               release.

  HOST-NET-031   P0             §607           Netsons profile sections
                                               override conflicting
                                               generic infrastructure
                                               recommendations without
                                               relaxing product/security
                                               requirements.
