## Backend portability and zero-touch backend transition

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  ID                     Priority               Requirement
  ---------------------- ---------------------- -------------------------
  PORT-001               P0                     Worker is
                                                provider/backend
                                                agnostic; hosting
                                                migration alone requires
                                                no reinstall or re-pair.

  PORT-002               P0                     Worker uses stable Kivro
                                                service discovery/naming,
                                                not permanent
                                                provider-specific
                                                endpoints.

  PORT-003               P0                     Kivro Worker Protocol is
                                                explicitly versioned
                                                independently from
                                                backend
                                                releases/providers.

  PORT-004               P0                     Previously paired Worker
                                                identity/credentials
                                                remain valid after
                                                authorized backend
                                                migration.

  PORT-005               P0                     Each execution has
                                                immutable control-plane
                                                ownership and lease
                                                identity.

  PORT-006               P0                     OLD=DRAINING and
                                                NEW=ACTIVE backends may
                                                coexist during a backend
                                                transition.

  PORT-007               P0                     DRAINING backend
                                                server-side rejects
                                                dispatch of new seller
                                                jobs after cutover.

  PORT-008               P0                     Worker with old running
                                                jobs may temporarily
                                                connect to both old and
                                                new backends.

  PORT-009               P0                     Idle Worker migrates to
                                                ACTIVE backend and closes
                                                obsolete connection.

  PORT-010               P0                     Global IDs, leases and
                                                idempotency prevent
                                                duplicate execution and
                                                financial effects across
                                                backends.

  PORT-011               P1                     Control plane models
                                                ACTIVE/DRAINING/RETIRED
                                                migration states.

  PORT-012               P0                     Netsons-to-AWS migration
                                                is zero-touch for sellers
                                                under normal operation.

  PORT-013               P0                     Migration is
                                                rollback-aware and never
                                                duplicates an execution
                                                as recovery.

  PORT-014               P1                     Worker software-update
                                                channel is independent
                                                from application-backend
                                                migration.

  PORT-015               P0                     Backend-transition
                                                observability proves
                                                old-backend new
                                                dispatch=0 and old owned
                                                work drains to 0.

  PORT-016               P0                     E2E proves one paired
                                                Worker can finish
                                                old-backend work while
                                                accepting new-backend
                                                work without duplicates.

  PORT-017               P1                     Worker caches
                                                last-known-good discovery
                                                with bounded
                                                revalidation.

  PORT-018               P0                     Protocol compatibility
                                                negotiation permits
                                                backend replacement while
                                                protocol support
                                                overlaps.
  -----------------------------------------------------------------------

## Transport-independent Worker connectivity

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  ID                    Priority              Requirement
  --------------------- --------------------- ---------------------------
  PORT-019              P0                    Worker Protocol application
                                              semantics must be
                                              independent from network
                                              transport.

  PORT-020              P0                    Worker must implement
                                              polling and WebSocket as
                                              separate transport adapters
                                              beneath one shared Worker
                                              state machine/protocol.

  PORT-021              P0                    Service discovery must
                                              advertise supported
                                              transport type(s) and
                                              versioned transport
                                              connection parameters per
                                              control plane.

  PORT-022              P0                    One Worker must support
                                              simultaneous polling to one
                                              control plane and WebSocket
                                              to another without
                                              cross-routing executions.

  PORT-023              P0                    Transport instances and
                                              messages must be scoped to
                                              controlPlaneId and
                                              immutable execution
                                              ownership.

  PORT-024              P0                    Polling and WebSocket must
                                              preserve equivalent
                                              authorization, lease,
                                              idempotency, result and
                                              financial semantics.

  PORT-025              P0                    Polling adapter must
                                              implement bounded HTTPS
                                              polling,
                                              retry/backoff/jitter,
                                              timeout, idempotent
                                              acknowledgement and
                                              reconciliation.

  PORT-026              P0                    WebSocket adapter must
                                              implement authenticated
                                              WSS, liveness,
                                              reconnect/backoff/jitter,
                                              reconciliation and
                                              replay-safe handling.

  PORT-027              P1                    When multiple transports
                                              are advertised,
                                              selection/fallback must be
                                              deterministic, explicit and
                                              security-preserving.

  PORT-028              P0                    Transport failure/reconnect
                                              must never by itself
                                              duplicate, cancel, fail or
                                              financially settle an
                                              execution.

  PORT-029              P0                    E2E must prove
                                              polling+DRAINING and
                                              WSS+ACTIVE coexist for one
                                              paired Worker with
                                              exactly-once
                                              execution/financial
                                              effects.

  PORT-030              P1                    Architecture must not
                                              hard-code polling as old or
                                              WebSocket as new;
                                              transports remain generally
                                              interchangeable.
  -----------------------------------------------------------------------

## Unified monorepo / dual deployment profile requirements

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  ID                     Priority               Requirement
  ---------------------- ---------------------- -------------------------
  ARCH-UNI-001           P0                     Kivro Cloud must be one
                                                shared TypeScript/Node
                                                application/domain
                                                implementation consumed
                                                by both deployment
                                                profiles.

  ARCH-UNI-002           P0                     Netsons and AWS cloud
                                                entrypoints must be
                                                composition
                                                roots/adapters, not
                                                independent copies of
                                                Kivro business logic.

  ARCH-UNI-003           P0                     Provider-neutral business
                                                rules, state machines,
                                                validation, API
                                                semantics, payment logic
                                                and Worker Protocol must
                                                exist exactly once in
                                                shared packages.

  ARCH-UNI-004           P0                     Shared/core packages must
                                                not import Netsons- or
                                                AWS-specific
                                                infrastructure packages.

  ARCH-UNI-005           P0                     Infrastructure-specific
                                                behavior must implement
                                                shared ports/interfaces
                                                owned by the core side.

  ARCH-UNI-006           P0                     Repository must contain
                                                both Netsons and AWS
                                                deployment profiles from
                                                the initial greenfield
                                                implementation.

  ARCH-UNI-007           P0                     Netsons baseline Worker
                                                transport is HTTPS
                                                polling implemented as an
                                                adapter to the shared
                                                Worker Protocol.

  ARCH-UNI-008           P0                     AWS baseline Worker
                                                transport is
                                                authenticated secure
                                                WebSocket implemented as
                                                an adapter to the shared
                                                Worker Protocol.

  ARCH-UNI-009           P0                     One provider-independent
                                                backend conformance suite
                                                must execute against both
                                                composition roots.

  ARCH-UNI-010           P0                     CI must block merge when
                                                either supported
                                                deployment profile fails
                                                shared conformance.

  ARCH-UNI-011           P0                     Public API
                                                schemas/contracts must be
                                                generated from one shared
                                                source and remain
                                                behaviorally equal across
                                                profiles.

  ARCH-UNI-012           P0                     Kivro must have one
                                                logical PostgreSQL data
                                                model and one
                                                schema/migration history;
                                                provider-specific schema
                                                forks are prohibited.

  ARCH-UNI-013           P0                     Financial, authorization,
                                                permission, idempotency
                                                and execution-ownership
                                                semantics must be
                                                identical across
                                                profiles.

  ARCH-UNI-014           P0                     Feature and bug-fix
                                                workflow must classify
                                                changes as shared vs
                                                infrastructure-specific
                                                and prohibit copy-based
                                                duplicate backend
                                                implementations.

  ARCH-UNI-015           P0                     CI must enforce
                                                dependency boundaries
                                                preventing shared
                                                packages from depending
                                                on provider-specific
                                                packages.

  ARCH-UNI-016           P1                     CI should detect
                                                prohibited duplicate core
                                                use-case implementations
                                                or equivalent
                                                architecture drift.

  ARCH-UNI-017           P0                     Both profiles must remain
                                                buildable/testable for
                                                every backend-affecting
                                                release even when only
                                                one is currently deployed
                                                in production.

  ARCH-UNI-018           P0                     A profile may become
                                                ACTIVE only after
                                                real-infrastructure smoke
                                                tests and full staging
                                                E2E pass for the release
                                                candidate.

  ARCH-UNI-019           P0                     Netsons and AWS
                                                deployment
                                                pipelines/configuration
                                                must remain independent
                                                while identifying the
                                                same shared application
                                                release.

  ARCH-UNI-020           P0                     Changing ACTIVE provider
                                                must be an operational
                                                control-plane transition,
                                                not an application
                                                rewrite or codebase
                                                migration.

  ARCH-UNI-021           P0                     Coverage evidence must
                                                identify whether each
                                                requirement is satisfied
                                                by shared code, Netsons
                                                adapter, AWS adapter, or
                                                a combination.

  ARCH-UNI-022           P0                     Unified architecture E2E
                                                must prove simultaneous
                                                Netsons polling/DRAINING
                                                and AWS WSS/ACTIVE using
                                                one paired Worker without
                                                reinstall/re-pair.

  ARCH-UNI-023           P0                     A deliberately introduced
                                                provider behavioral
                                                divergence must be
                                                detected by automated
                                                conformance/contract
                                                tests.

  ARCH-UNI-024           P0                     Schema/protocol releases
                                                must declare
                                                compatibility windows
                                                sufficient for
                                                simultaneous control
                                                planes during
                                                transitions.
  -----------------------------------------------------------------------

## Engineering hardening requirements

  ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ID                   Priority             Requirement
  -------------------- -------------------- ------------------------------
  HARD-001             P0                   CI must enforce architecture
                                            fitness rules preventing
                                            shared/core packages from
                                            importing provider-specific
                                            adapters/SDKs/composition
                                            roots.

  HARD-002             P0                   Public API contract, Worker
                                            Protocol source and logical
                                            database schema/migration
                                            history must each have one
                                            authoritative shared source.

  HARD-003             P0                   Maintain provider-independent
                                            golden behavioral scenarios
                                            and run the same scenarios
                                            against both Netsons and AWS
                                            composition roots.

  HARD-004             P0                   Golden scenarios must cover
                                            successful and failed paid
                                            jobs with exactly-once
                                            execution and financial
                                            effects.

  HARD-005             P0                   Maintain fault-injection tests
                                            for ambiguous DB
                                            commit/response loss,
                                            transport loss, duplicate
                                            completion, duplicate
                                            webhook/task execution and
                                            process restart.

  HARD-006             P0                   Fault-injection tests must
                                            assert converged authoritative
                                            job, ledger, payment, asset
                                            and execution state.

  HARD-007             P0                   Cloud, Worker, Worker
                                            Protocol, API, DB schema and
                                            capability manifest versions
                                            must be independently
                                            identifiable and governed by
                                            an explicit compatibility
                                            matrix.

  HARD-008             P0                   Unsupported Worker/protocol
                                            combinations must fail closed
                                            with actionable sanitized
                                            health information.

  HARD-009             P0                   Database evolution must follow
                                            expand/deploy/contract
                                            discipline across any
                                            overlapping control-plane
                                            compatibility window.

  HARD-010             P0                   Schema backfills/migrations
                                            used during compatible
                                            rollouts must be idempotent or
                                            safely resumable.

  HARD-011             P0                   Worker update
                                            artifacts/metadata must be
                                            cryptographically signed and
                                            verified before installation.

  HARD-012             P1                   Worker update mechanism must
                                            support staged rollout, health
                                            verification and
                                            rollback/recovery where
                                            technically possible.

  HARD-013             P0                   Backend/provider transition
                                            alone must not force a Worker
                                            binary update when
                                            protocol/transport
                                            compatibility already exists.

  HARD-014             P0                   Production readiness must
                                            define measurable
                                            PostgreSQL/object-state RPO
                                            and RTO targets and documented
                                            recovery procedures.

  HARD-015             P0                   Backups are not considered
                                            verified until an isolated
                                            restore test validates
                                            integrity and critical Kivro
                                            invariants.

  HARD-016             P0                   Loss of Redis/cache must not
                                            cause loss of authoritative
                                            jobs, payments, ledger,
                                            capability or Worker identity
                                            state.

  HARD-017             P0                   Maintain a
                                            threat→control→test→evidence
                                            matrix for material threats
                                            defined by the Master Spec.

  HARD-018             P0                   Security release audit must
                                            require evidence for sandbox,
                                            SSRF/network, credentials,
                                            payment replay, control-plane
                                            routing, asset and
                                            Worker-version threats.

  HARD-019             P1                   Define measurable MVP
                                            performance/resource envelopes
                                            for API, assets, Worker
                                            connectivity, leases/timeouts,
                                            queues, research, inference
                                            and webhooks before go-live.

  HARD-020             P1                   Load/resilience tests must
                                            validate the selected
                                            production envelope for each
                                            deployment profile without
                                            changing shared semantics.

  HARD-021             P0                   Every releasable build must
                                            emit a machine-readable
                                            unified release manifest
                                            covering application,
                                            protocol, API, schema and
                                            adapter compatibility.

  HARD-022             P0                   Generate provider parity
                                            evidence from
                                            requirements/tests and block
                                            release/activation on missing
                                            required parity.

  HARD-023             P0                   Every backend-affecting PR
                                            must run architecture fitness,
                                            shared tests, both provider
                                            conformance suites and
                                            relevant profile tests.

  HARD-024             P0                   Each implementation milestone
                                            must define/inherit
                                            preconditions, deliverables,
                                            forbidden shortcuts, required
                                            tests, evidence and objective
                                            exit criteria.

  HARD-025             P0                   CI must contain a drift canary
                                            proving that an intentionally
                                            non-conformant adapter is
                                            rejected by conformance tests.

  HARD-026             P0                   Worker restart during
                                            simultaneous polling/WSS
                                            control-plane coexistence must
                                            preserve execution ownership
                                            and converge without duplicate
                                            effects.

  HARD-027             P0                   Release activation must verify
                                            schema/protocol compatibility
                                            with any still-supported
                                            ACTIVE/DRAINING control plane
                                            and Worker population.

  HARD-028             P1                   Operational dashboards/alerts
                                            must expose parity/conformance
                                            release status, durable
                                            backlog age, Worker version
                                            health and reconciliation
                                            anomalies without exposing
                                            seller secrets.
  ------------------------------------------------------------------------

## Durable asynchronous job/result requirements

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  ID                  Priority            Requirement
  ------------------- ------------------- -------------------------------
  ASYNC-001           P0                  Jobs are durable asynchronous
                                          resources independent of
                                          buyer/seller browser session
                                          lifetime.

  ASYNC-002           P0                  Buyer may disconnect after
                                          committed job creation/payment
                                          reservation and later retrieve
                                          status/result from another
                                          authenticated session.

  ASYNC-003           P0                  Shared Core persists all state
                                          needed to reconstruct
                                          non-terminal immediate, queued
                                          and scheduled jobs after cloud
                                          restart.

  ASYNC-004           P0                  Buyer textual/structured inputs
                                          required for execution are
                                          durably persisted before
                                          dispatch.

  ASYNC-005           P0                  Buyer input files use private
                                          Kivro-controlled object storage
                                          with durable PostgreSQL asset
                                          metadata.

  ASYNC-006           P0                  Job cannot become buyer-visible
                                          COMPLETED until declared
                                          outputs are validated and
                                          durably finalized by Kivro.

  ASYNC-007           P0                  Accepted output files persist
                                          in private Kivro-controlled
                                          object storage; Worker/cloud
                                          local filesystem is not durable
                                          delivery storage.

  ASYNC-008           P0                  Accepted textual/structured
                                          outputs and output metadata are
                                          durably persisted.

  ASYNC-009           P0                  Completed result retrieval is
                                          independent of seller Worker
                                          availability after
                                          finalization.

  ASYNC-010           P0                  Buyer UI provides durable My
                                          Jobs/Purchases history and
                                          persistent job/result view.

  ASYNC-011           P0                  Result view exposes safe
                                          status/schedule/results/files
                                          and applicable retention/expiry
                                          information.

  ASYNC-012           P0                  Output objects are private and
                                          every download is re-authorized
                                          before short-lived access is
                                          issued.

  ASYNC-013           P0                  Presigned/scoped download URLs
                                          are short-lived and regenerable
                                          during retention.

  ASYNC-014           P0                  Authorization tests cover
                                          anonymous, cross-buyer,
                                          cross-seller, expired-link and
                                          object-key enumeration
                                          attempts.

  ASYNC-015           P0                  Configurable input/output asset
                                          retention policy is defined
                                          before production.

  ASYNC-016           P0                  Retention semantics are shared
                                          across Netsons and AWS.

  ASYNC-017           P0                  Cleanup is asynchronous,
                                          idempotent, auditable and
                                          converges safely across
                                          DB/object-store partial
                                          failure.

  ASYNC-018           P1                  Buyer UI/API exposes expiry
                                          when deliverable assets have
                                          finite retention.

  ASYNC-019           P0                  Kivro durably records terminal
                                          notification work; buyer need
                                          not keep result page open.

  ASYNC-020           P1                  MVP supports email notification
                                          for relevant completed/failed
                                          outcomes subject to
                                          preferences.

  ASYNC-021           P0                  Notification failure does not
                                          invalidate/rollback completed
                                          job; durable history is
                                          authoritative.

  ASYNC-022           P0                  Buyer webhooks provide
                                          retry-safe machine-client
                                          terminal notifications.

  ASYNC-023           P0                  Scheduled jobs retain cloud
                                          state/input assets until
                                          eligible seller execution
                                          without buyer presence.

  ASYNC-024           P0                  Netsons polling and AWS WSS
                                          expose identical scheduled-job
                                          and durable-result semantics.

  ASYNC-025           P0                  Application uses shared
                                          object-storage semantics and
                                          never cloud local filesystem
                                          persistence for buyer
                                          deliverables.

  ASYNC-026           P0                  Completion/finalization is
                                          idempotent under duplicate
                                          completion, lost
                                          acknowledgement, restart and
                                          reconnect.

  ASYNC-027           P0                  Replayed completion cannot
                                          duplicate logical assets,
                                          charges, seller earnings or
                                          settlement.

  ASYNC-028           P0                  Reconciliation detects
                                          missing/corrupt required output
                                          objects referenced by completed
                                          state.

  ASYNC-029           P0                  Buyer REST API exposes same
                                          durable status/result semantics
                                          and authorized short-lived file
                                          access as web UI.

  ASYNC-030           P0                  Backend conformance suite runs
                                          durable asynchronous result
                                          scenarios against both Netsons
                                          and AWS roots.
  -----------------------------------------------------------------------

## Buyer experience requirements

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ID              Priority        Requirement
  --------------- --------------- ---------------------------------------------------------
  BUYERUX-001     P0              Pre-purchase preflight checks known hard readiness
                                  blockers without starting paid execution.

  BUYERUX-002     P0              Execution quote shows immutable price authorization,
                                  availability, expected/eligible start, queue/schedule
                                  context, completion range when evidence exists, and quote
                                  expiry.

  BUYERUX-003     P0              Quote is atomically revalidated at purchase against
                                  current
                                  price/version/availability/security/deadline/payment
                                  state.

  BUYERUX-004     P0              Buyer may set supported hard delivery deadline or maximum
                                  wait, persisted as shared scheduling constraints.

  BUYERUX-005     P0              Purchase flow rejects/warns when current evidence makes
                                  requested deadline infeasible; estimates are not
                                  mislabeled guarantees.

  BUYERUX-006     P0              Marketplace Agent honors the same hard deadline
                                  constraints.

  BUYERUX-007     P0              Buyer can cancel eligible non-started scheduled/queued
                                  job with idempotent dispatch prevention and credit
                                  release/refund.

  BUYERUX-008     P0              Cancel-vs-claim race is resolved atomically server-side
                                  with clear buyer policy.

  BUYERUX-009     P1              Capabilities may expose versioned meaningful progress
                                  stages; Kivro persists structured safe progress.

  BUYERUX-010     P0              Progress never exposes chain-of-thought, secrets, host
                                  paths, seller-private resource details or unsafe raw
                                  logs.

  BUYERUX-011     P1              Kivro may show dynamic estimated start/completion ranges
                                  from trustworthy schedule/queue/runtime history.

  BUYERUX-012     P0              ETA uses honest ranges/unknown state and never fabricates
                                  precision.

  BUYERUX-013     P1              Buyer has durable notification preferences for
                                  completion/failure and optional start events.

  BUYERUX-014     P0              Human notifications contain no permanent public asset
                                  URLs or unnecessary sensitive result/input data.

  BUYERUX-015     P1              Completed/history jobs support Run again via a new
                                  independently quoted/authorized job.

  BUYERUX-016     P1              History supports Duplicate & edit with reusable inputs
                                  and explicit replacement of expired/deleted assets.

  BUYERUX-017     P0              Rerun/duplicate never assumes historical
                                  price/version/availability/permissions are still current.

  BUYERUX-018     P0              Result UI uses output contract to render a semantic
                                  deliverable manifest rather than an unstructured file
                                  list.

  BUYERUX-019     P1              Safe supported outputs can be previewed inline without
                                  making private objects public.

  BUYERUX-020     P0              Active/unsafe preview content is sandboxed/sanitized;
                                  unsupported formats fall back to authorized download.

  BUYERUX-021     P1              Multi-file results offer bounded authorized Download all
                                  packaging where practical.

  BUYERUX-022     P0              Bundle generation prevents malicious filenames/path
                                  traversal/symlink attacks and does not block individual
                                  downloads on failure.

  BUYERUX-023     P0              Historical job displays immutable/sanitized input
                                  snapshot; expired binaries may retain metadata only.

  BUYERUX-024     P0              Purchased job preserves immutable
                                  capability/version/seller/price/I-O/permission/schedule
                                  purchase snapshot.

  BUYERUX-025     P0              Seller missing an allowed execution window becomes
                                  explicit delayed/missed state rather than indefinite
                                  silent scheduling.

  BUYERUX-026     P0              Missed-window UX exposes legal choices such as
                                  wait-next-window or cancel/release/refund according to
                                  shared policy.

  BUYERUX-027     P0              Every scheduled/queued paid job has bounded
                                  wait/start-expiry policy.

  BUYERUX-028     P0              Exceeded hard latest-start/expiry prevents future
                                  execution and applies idempotent release/refund safely
                                  against concurrent claim.

  BUYERUX-029     P1              Marketplace surfaces buyer-useful seller reliability
                                  metrics computed from authoritative platform history.

  BUYERUX-030     P0              Reliability definitions/windows/sample thresholds/failure
                                  attribution are documented and insufficient history is
                                  explicit.

  BUYERUX-031     P0              Reliability metrics never expose buyer-private
                                  information.

  BUYERUX-032     P1              Finite result retention is clearly shown with
                                  expiry/remaining availability and optional pre-expiry
                                  reminder.

  BUYERUX-033     P1              Completed result offers Looks good / Report a problem or
                                  equivalent durable acknowledgement/problem flow.

  BUYERUX-034     P0              Problem report links to immutable job/purchase/output
                                  snapshot without silently changing settlement or creating
                                  escrow.

  BUYERUX-035     P0              Before purchase buyer sees truthful disclosure of which
                                  submitted information may reach seller execution
                                  environment.

  BUYERUX-036     P0              Buyer is told that payment credentials/unrelated account
                                  data are not shared with seller by default, matching
                                  implementation.

  BUYERUX-037     P0              Worker receives minimum required buyer data using
                                  opaque/pseudonymous job identifiers by default.

  BUYERUX-038     P0              Buyer email/payment/unrelated profile data is not sent to
                                  Worker unless an explicitly declared input/permission
                                  genuinely requires it.

  BUYERUX-039     P0              Seller disappearance cannot trap non-started paid jobs
                                  indefinitely; max-wait/deadline drives choices and
                                  automatic cancellation.

  BUYERUX-040     P0              Seller disappearance after cloud-finalized completion
                                  cannot affect result retrieval.

  BUYERUX-041     P1              Buyer home/dashboard summarizes scheduled, queued,
                                  running/paused, newly completed, failed/problematic and
                                  expiring-result work.

  BUYERUX-042     P0              Buyer dashboard derives from authoritative shared job
                                  state and is provider-neutral.

  BUYERUX-043     P0              Buyer async UX clearly communicates purchase, payment,
                                  start/wait reason, ETA/unknown, cancelability, progress,
                                  outputs, retention and problem path.

  BUYERUX-044     P0              Kivro forbids fake progress, fake ETA certainty, hidden
                                  indefinite waits, ambiguous payment state and
                                  pre-finalization success UI.

  BUYERUX-045     P0              All buyer-experience semantics are shared Core behavior
                                  and pass equivalent Netsons/AWS conformance scenarios.
  -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

## Seller experience requirements

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ID                      Priority                Requirement
  ----------------------- ----------------------- -----------------------------------------------------------
  SELLERUX-001            P0                      Guided Worker onboarding verifies pairing, OpenClaw
                                                  compatibility, sandbox, connectivity and prerequisites with
                                                  actionable blocking/warning states.

  SELLERUX-002            P0                      Worker discovers candidate skills/tools/resources
                                                  read-only; discovery never auto-exposes or auto-publishes
                                                  anything.

  SELLERUX-003            P0                      Normal capability creation uses a progressive safe wizard
                                                  rather than requiring raw manifests.

  SELLERUX-004            P1                      Platform AI may draft listing/schema/dependency/test
                                                  suggestions but cannot grant permissions, choose secrets,
                                                  price, publish or expand access without explicit seller
                                                  approval.

  SELLERUX-005            P0                      Dependency auto-detection produces seller-reviewable
                                                  graph/closure with uncertainty and fails closed on
                                                  unresolved mandatory dependencies.

  SELLERUX-006            P0                      Seller reviews human-readable effective permissions derived
                                                  from authoritative machine policy before publish.

  SELLERUX-007            P1                      Seller can Preview as buyer using draft contract without
                                                  making draft purchasable.

  SELLERUX-008            P0                      Publish readiness uses deterministic blocking/recommended
                                                  checks, not arbitrary vanity scoring.

  SELLERUX-009            P0                      Seller execution secrets use local/approved secret storage
                                                  and are not uploaded to cloud for convenience.

  SELLERUX-010            P0                      Cloud stores only minimal non-secret credential
                                                  references/health metadata needed for orchestration.

  SELLERUX-011            P0                      Seller dashboard shows freshness-aware
                                                  credential/dependency health without exposing secret
                                                  values.

  SELLERUX-012            P0                      Seller configures always/custom recurring availability with
                                                  explicit timezone and next-window preview.

  SELLERUX-013            P0                      Seller can temporarily pause new jobs without creating a
                                                  new capability version.

  SELLERUX-014            P0                      Seller configures safe maximum concurrent job capacity;
                                                  shared slot reservation prevents over-dispatch.

  SELLERUX-015            P1                      Seller dashboard summarizes actionable jobs, capability
                                                  availability/health and earnings.

  SELLERUX-016            P0                      Seller job detail shows only execution-relevant declared
                                                  buyer inputs and safe operational/earning information.

  SELLERUX-017            P0                      Kivro shows estimated seller-funded provider cost and
                                                  estimated net proceeds before publish when estimable, with
                                                  uncertainty labeling.

  SELLERUX-018            P0                      Provider proxy/Worker enforces per-job
                                                  cost/model/token/request guardrails where technically
                                                  possible; buyer cannot override them.

  SELLERUX-019            P1                      Profit analytics distinguish gross sales, Kivro fees,
                                                  seller earnings, measured/estimated provider costs and
                                                  estimated net.

  SELLERUX-020            P0                      Earnings dashboard derives pending/available/paid and
                                                  payout status from authoritative ledger/Stripe state, never
                                                  Worker self-report.

  SELLERUX-021            P1                      Capability analytics expose reliable
                                                  funnel/execution/reliability/revenue metrics without
                                                  buyer-private leakage.

  SELLERUX-022            P0                      Capability edits create draft version; seller sees semantic
                                                  LIVE-vs-draft diff including security-sensitive permission
                                                  expansion.

  SELLERUX-023            P0                      Publishing new version never mutates historical or pinned
                                                  jobs.

  SELLERUX-024            P0                      Seller can stop new jobs on bad version and restore an
                                                  eligible prior published version without rewriting
                                                  in-flight/history.

  SELLERUX-025            P0                      Rollback is blocked when prior version violates current
                                                  security/platform compatibility and reason is explained.

  SELLERUX-026            P0                      Deterministic health policy can auto-pause
                                                  unsafe/unready/repeatedly failing capability from new
                                                  purchases/dispatch.

  SELLERUX-027            P0                      Auto-pause exposes exact reason/evidence/remediation/retest
                                                  path and is not based on opaque LLM judgment.

  SELLERUX-028            P1                      Seller notification preferences cover
                                                  Worker/health/failure/missed-window/problem-report/payout
                                                  events with dedup/rate limits.

  SELLERUX-029            P0                      Maintenance mode prevents new work while marketplace
                                                  truthfully exposes maintenance/known return time.

  SELLERUX-030            P0                      Seller UI explains precise composable unavailability
                                                  blockers and remediation rather than generic Offline when
                                                  known.

  SELLERUX-031            P0                      Worker Doctor/UI diagnostics validate runtime,
                                                  connectivity, sandbox, resources, network broker,
                                                  credentials, dependencies and manifest safely.

  SELLERUX-032            P0                      Diagnostic/support output is sanitized and cannot leak
                                                  secrets or buyer-private data.

  SELLERUX-033            P0                      Seller-facing security disclosure accurately explains buyer
                                                  isolation and capability-selected local resource access.

  SELLERUX-034            P0                      Public seller display identity may differ from private
                                                  Stripe/KYC legal identity subject to policy.

  SELLERUX-035            P0                      Legal/KYC identity is not exposed publicly by default
                                                  merely because Kivro stores it for payout compliance.

  SELLERUX-036            P0                      Domain model is future-compatible with multiple Workers per
                                                  seller and does not encode seller=singleton Worker.

  SELLERUX-037            P0                      Capability/Worker assignment and routing are explicit
                                                  enough to support multiple Workers without destructive
                                                  redesign.

  SELLERUX-038            P1                      Seller can inspect major disclosed reliability/reputation
                                                  contributors and definitions without exposing
                                                  anti-abuse-sensitive internals.

  SELLERUX-039            P1                      Post-publish first-sale onboarding guides listing preview,
                                                  examples, payout/readiness, availability and optional
                                                  sharing.

  SELLERUX-040            P1                      After first sale/run Kivro explains outcome, earning,
                                                  fee/cost context, settlement/payout timing and monitoring
                                                  path.

  SELLERUX-041            P0                      Seller experience business semantics are shared and pass
                                                  equivalent Netsons/AWS conformance scenarios.
  -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
